They can push this to nearly two bits per value, but rarely further, because quality drops sharply at this 2-bit cliff: four levels are too few for the cache's outlier-heavy values, where a few large entries consume the levels and collapse the rest into noise. A natural remedy is to spend more bits on the channels (feature dimensions) that matter and fewer on the rest, but the raw cache offers no handle: its channels are strongly correlated, so none stands out as more important.
  Our analysis shows that this handle appears once the cache is rotated into a coordinate system computed from its own statistics, removing these correlations. There, a small fraction of channels carries almost all the information, and spending the budget on those few is far more accurate than spreading it evenly.
  Guided by this analysis, we develop SPECTRA, a training-free, drop-in codec that re-encodes the cache into this coordinate system and concentrates the bit budget on the channels that carry the signal. On Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B over long-context benchmarks, SPECTRA is near-lossless at 4x compression, competitive at 8x where uniform quantization has collapsed, and reaches up to 12x, pushing usable compression past the 2-bit cliff so the same GPU holds longer contexts and larger batches.

</details>


### [134] [Spectral Outliers Reveal Dominant Learned Structure in Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07921)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva,T. H. Bandara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力权重进行"随机体"与"谱离群值"的分解，并通过因果实验验证该分解的有效性：在Mistral-7B中将MP识别的离群值置零会导致HellaSwag、MMLU和PIQA接近随机水平，而置零同等数量的体奇异值则影响较小。在11个预训练Transformer中发现五种重复出现的谱模式，并据此提出对参数高效微调和结构化剪枝的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预训练注意力权重中哪些成分承载了真正学到的结构信息（信号）与哪些成分是随机噪声，对于模型可解释性和剪枝/微调策略至关重要。随机矩阵理论提供了一种无需监督信号即可区分信号与噪声的天然框架。

Method: 将Marchenko-Pastur分布应用于预训练注意力投影矩阵，将每个矩阵分解为随机状的体（bulk）和谱离群值（outliers）两个部分；通过因果验证（置零离群值或体奇异值）在Mistral-7B上评估分解效果；并在11个预训练Transformer上系统性地分析离群值的分布模式，包括按投影类型（Q/K/V/O）、注意力机制（GQA vs MHA）和残差流维度进行分类。

Result: (1) 离群值编码了学习结构的主要成分；(2) Q投影携带最多离群值；(3) GQA下的V投影缺乏清晰的信号/噪声分离；(4) 条目级离群值在Q中形成行带、在O中形成列带的结构化模式；(5) 特定残差流维度在K和O中跨层持续作为带状离群值存在。

Conclusion: 谱离群值是预训练注意力权重的核心结构载体，MP分解为理解Transformer权重提供了可靠的因果验证框架，可为参数高效微调和结构化剪枝提供理论依据和操作指导。

Abstract: We apply Marchenko-Pastur (MP) random matrix theory to pre-trained attention weights in order to separate each projection matrix into a random-like bulk and a set of spectral outliers. We validate this decomposition causally: zeroing the MP-identified outliers (signal) in Mistral-7B drives HellaSwag, MMLU, and PIQA close to random-chance performance, whereas zeroing a count-matched subset of bulk singular values causes smaller but non-negligible degradation. Across 11 pre-trained transformers we identify five recurring patterns: spectral outliers encode a dominant component of the learned structure; Q projections carry the most outliers; V projections under grouped-query attention lack a clean signal/noise separation; entry-level outliers form structured row-bands in Q and column-bands in O; and specific residual-stream dimensions persist as band outliers across layers in K and O. We close by outlining how these observations could inform parameter-efficient fine-tuning and structured pruning.

</details>


### [135] [Information Routing across Batch Boundaries: Memory--Batch Tradeoffs in Lipschitz Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07922)
*Zicheng Lyu,Zengfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机Lipschitz bandit中"宽度-深度"权衡问题：学习者每轮最多保留W比特的奖励相关状态，且将拉取动作组织为最多B个批次。当W≳d log(eT)时，作者刻画了极小极大伪遗憾的精确阶（至多差对数因子），下界对所有W成立。发现了一个新的代价项 T^{(d+2)/(d+3)}(1+(B-1)W)^{-1/(d(d+3))}，证明状态宽度与更新深度不可互换，二者交互本质是信息路由约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应学习既需要保留观测含义的状态，也需要作用于该状态的机会。现有工作分别研究了完全序列化（无内存限制）和批次化（无内存限制）两种情形，但实际学习系统通常同时受限于内存（状态宽度W）和批次频率（更新深度B），二者的联合影响尚未被理解。

Method: 作者建立了问题依赖（problem-dependent）的信息论分析与匹配的策略构造。理论上，通过信息路由约束证明：在区域尺度s上，低遗憾迫使提交的动作转录需编码Θ_d(s^{-d})个区域决策，而收集的边界状态最多携带(B-1)W比特熵，由此导出新的下界。策略层面，采用流式擦除验证统计量、同时保留安全活动集掩码的方法，掩码可整体保存在内存中或逐片段保存，实现了匹配的上界。定理统一恢复了全维度最坏情况批次-only前沿以及完全序列化特例下的对数内存可实现性。

Result: 1) 证明了当W≥c·d·log(eT)时，极小极大期望伪遗憾的精确阶（忽略对数因子），下界对任意W成立。2) 发现经典序列化与无限制内存批次代价之间存在新边界项，体现宽度与深度不可互换。3) 静态批次边界与可预测自适应边界的结果一致。

Conclusion: 本研究首次从理论上刻画了记忆宽度（W）与批次数（B）对随机Lipschitz bandit学习性能的联合影响。核心发现是宽度和深度并非可互换的资源：信息路由约束决定了两者的耦合效应。研究的方法论（流式验证统计与掩码技术）为兼具内存与通信约束的bandit算法设计提供了新范式，其结论同时涵盖了批次-only和序列化两种经典极端情形。

Abstract: Adaptive learning needs both a state that preserves what observations imply and opportunities to act on that state. We study this width--depth tradeoff in stochastic Lipschitz bandits. After each pull, the learner retains at most $W$ bits of live reward-dependent state and organizes its pulls into at most $B$ committed batches. For $W\gtrsim_d\log(eT)$, we characterize minimax expected pseudo-regret up to logarithmic factors; the lower bounds hold for every $W$. Besides the classical sequential and unrestricted-memory batch costs, the frontier contains the new penalty \[
  T^{\frac{d+2}{d+3}}
  \bigl(1+(B-1)W\bigr)^{-\frac1{d(d+3)}}, \] proving that state width and update depth are not interchangeable. The interaction is an information-routing constraint: at regional scale $s$, low regret forces the committed action transcript to encode $Θ_d(s^{-d})$ regional decisions, while the collected boundary states carry at most $(B-1)W$ bits of entropy. Matching policies stream and erase verification statistics while retaining a mask of a safe active set, either in memory or fragment by fragment. The theorem recovers the full-dimensional worst-case batch-only frontier and logarithmic-memory achievability in the fully sequential specialization; static batch boundaries match predictable adaptive ones.

</details>


### [136] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出二阶漂移模型（Second-Order Drifting Models），通过将漂移动力学提升到相空间并引入人工速度变量，实现对傅里叶空间中密度扰动的高阶加速动力学，从而缓解一阶漂移模型在精细结构恢复上的谱刚度问题，同时保持单步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶漂移模型虽然避免了迭代推理，但其基于核函数的漂移场导致频率相关的训练动力学：在线性化机制下，每个傅里叶模态的密度残差衰减速率由核谱决定，导致精细尺度结构的恢复缓慢。这种谱刚度问题限制了模型的收敛效率。

Method: 将漂移动力学提升到相空间，为生成的样本引入人工速度变量，使密度扰动满足傅里叶空间中的加速二阶动力学，与优化理论中的Nesterov加速方法建立联系。据此推导出实用的半隐式训练算法。

Result: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制等多个任务上，二阶漂移模型改善了收敛行为，性能达到或超过一阶漂移基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过将二阶加速动力学引入漂移模型，提供了一种缓解一阶漂移谱刚度问题的原理性机制，在不牺牲单步推理的前提下显著提升收敛性能。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>


### [137] [Adaptive Supervised Anchoring for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07935)
*Meilin Yang,Zixuan Ding,Jianhao Nie,Weite Zhang,Yuxin Zhang,Zhiming Shao,Li Yu,Zhe Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出启发式策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，当学生轨迹偏离目标时，教师模型的条件信号会退化。作者提出一个统一训练框架，将轨迹条件的分布匹配与规范监督（标准真实上下文上的交叉熵）分离，并通过token级对齐动态调整规范锚点的强度，从而在多种模型规模和任务上获得更优的塑性-稳定性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法依赖学生采样的轨迹质量，但学生rollout偏离目标轨迹会导致教师模型在偏离前缀上的监督信号显著弱化，称为"rollout条件信号退化"。这一问题未被系统识别和处理，限制了OPSD的训练效果。

Method: 提出统一训练框架，包含两条互补的监督路径：（1）保留rollout条件分布匹配，为学生实际访问的状态提供指导；（2）在规范真实上下文中施加监督交叉熵，避免在错误rollout前缀上强加目标token。通过token级rollout-目标对齐来动态调节规范上下文锚点的强度：冷启动时加强，rollout质量改善后逐渐放松。

Result: 在多种模型规模、两类任务族和通用推理基准上，所提方法相较OPSD改善了任务习得效果，同时保持通用能力，呈现出更有利的经验性塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 上下文质量是启发式策略自蒸馏的核心瓶颈，将rollout条件指导与规范监督分离具有重要价值，为后续OPSD类方法的改进提供了方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) adapts a language model by distilling guidance from a frozen teacher on trajectories sampled from the student. Its effectiveness, however, depends critically on the quality of those trajectories. We show that when student rollouts drift from target trajectories, conditioning the teacher on off-target prefixes substantially weakens its task-relevant supervision. Controlled prefix-corruption experiments expose this failure mode, which we term rollout-conditioned signal degradation. To address this problem, we propose a unified training framework that separates two complementary supervision pathways. The first retains rollout-conditioned distribution matching, providing guidance on states the student actually visits. The second applies supervised cross-entropy on canonical ground-truth contexts, avoiding the incompatibility of imposing target tokens on erroneous rollout prefixes. Token-level rollout-target alignment is used to adapt the strength of the canonical-context anchor, emphasizing it during cold start and relaxing it as rollout quality improves. Experiments across multiple model scales, two task families, and general-reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed approach improves task acquisition over OPSD while preserving general capabilities, resulting in a more favorable empirical plasticity-stability trade-off. These findings identify context quality as a central bottleneck in on-policy self-distillation and demonstrate the value of separating rollout-conditioned guidance from canonical supervision.

</details>


### [138] [Persistent Semantic Entities in Tool-Augmented LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07952)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将工具增强型LLM代理中的隐式跨会话状态形式化为"持久语义实体"(PSEs)，测试了11个家族的24个模型，发现所有模型均受污染影响（20-100%），其中偏好和指令污染持续不衰减且无法自校正，构成严重的安全攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM代理存在跨会话、跨边界传播的隐式状态，这些状态对标准调试手段几乎不可见，构成了严重的安全隐患，但缺乏系统性的形式化分析和评估。

Method: 形式化定义了持久语义实体(PSEs)的三个核心结构属性：名称绑定、事件触发和跨边界传播；在24个模型（1.5B-1T参数，11个家族）上进行系统性评估，并测试了上下文隔离的自验证检测方法和基于关键词的检测方法。

Result: 所有测试模型均易受PSE污染影响（20-100%）；名称绑定是必要且主导的机制（无它则污染率为0%）；偏好污染在所有模型上100%持续到t=10不衰减，指令污染持续存在，人格注入部分衰减（90%→10%），事实注入因模型而异；上下文隔离自验证可减少20-79%污染（中位数36.5%），关键词检测产生系统性误报；污染沿四阶段代理流水线累积1.9倍（40%→75%）。

Conclusion: 偏好和指令污染具有持久性、缺乏自校正能力且标准监控难以捕获，对部署中的代理系统构成特别严重的安全攻击面，需要更有效的检测和防御机制。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents can harbor implicit state that persists across sessions, activates through events, and propagates across agent boundaries---largely invisible to standard debugging. We formalize this as Persistent Semantic Entities (PSEs): constructs defined by name binding, event triggering, and cross-boundary propagation, and evaluate them across 24 models from 11 families (1.5B--1T parameters). First, every tested model is susceptible (20--100% on the 20-model susceptibility panel), with name binding as the necessary and dominant mechanism: without it, contamination is 0%. Second, persistence depends on contamination type rather than scale or deployment: preference contamination persists undecayed on every model probed (100% at t=10) and instruction contamination persists wherever adopted, persona-style injection decays partially (90%$\to$10%), while factual injection is model-dependent---self-corrected on Llama-3.1-8B and GPT-4o-mini but held at ceiling on both Qwen2.5-coder variants, so we do not claim it self-corrects in general. The preference and instruction results hold across providers in our controlled setting. Third, context-isolated self-verification achieves 20--79% reduction (median 36.5%) without oracle references while keyword-based detection produces systematic false positives, and contamination compounds 1.9$\times$ along a four-stage agent pipeline (40%$\to$75%). Preference and instruction contamination---persistent, lacking self-correction, and poorly captured by standard monitoring---represent a particularly concerning attack surface for deployed agent systems.

</details>


### [139] [From Uncertainty to Failure Attribution: Self-Diagnosing Models for Failure Attribution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07953)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的问题设定——分布偏移下的失败归因（failure attribution），并提出了自诊断模型（self-diagnosing models），该模型不仅检测OOD样本，还能通过失败归因向量区分四种失败类型（协变量偏移、语义偏移、噪声损坏和对抗扰动），将标量不确定性升级为失败识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布偏移解决方案仅能检测OOD样本或预测不确定性水平，但无法解释模型为何失败。作者希望通过失败归因设定，让模型不仅知道"何时失败"，还能知道"为何失败"，从而提升模型的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Method: 提出自诊断模型，联合学习三个目标：预测输出、预测不确定性和失败归因信号。核心组件包括：1）由神经网络生成的失败归因向量，对四种失败类型进行分类；2）一致性正则化器，用于鼓励不确定性预测与失败归因预测之间的一致性；3）构建具有预定义偏移生成机制的分布偏移基准数据集用于评估。

Result: 通过一致性正则化和四类失败归因向量的结构化表示，模型能够从标量不确定性预测扩展到细粒度的失败类型识别，并在所构建的分布偏移基准上评估其归因能力。

Conclusion: 本文从检测"是否分布偏移"迈向识别"分布偏移的类型"，为增强模型在分布偏移下的可解释性和可靠性提供了一个新的方向，通过失败归因向量实现了更细粒度的失败诊断。

Abstract: Distribution shift poses a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning models, but the current solutions only aim to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) samples and predict uncertainty levels. We introduce a problem setting for failure attribution under distribution shift, which enables the models not only to detect OOD samples, but also to find out the reason for their failure. The solution we propose is called self-diagnosing models, which are capable of jointly learning predictive output, predictive uncertainty, and a failure attribution signal. In particular, we use the failure attribution vector, produced by a neural network, which provides a structured representation of predictive unreliability by distinguishing four different types of failures: covariance shift, semantic shift, noise corruption, and adversarial perturbation. In other words, we move from scalar uncertainty towards failure identification. For training the model, we introduce a consistency regularizer that encourages consistency between uncertainty and failure attribution predictions. Moreover, to be able to evaluate the model on its ability to find the reasons for failure, we construct several distribution shift benchmarks with predefined mechanisms for generating distribution shifts.

</details>


### [140] [EasyBalance: Cross-Layer Load Balancing in Distributed MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07964)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyBalance 提出了一种跨层负载均衡策略，通过将不同 MoE 层的专家视为天然冗余，联合调度跨层工作负载来缓解单个层的路由不均衡，无需修改专家-设备映射且几乎零额外开销，在分布式 MoE 推理中减少了超过 40% 的 GPU 空闲时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行（expert-parallel）的分布式 MoE 模型推理中，路由分布通常偏斜，导致承载较轻负载的专家所对应的设备因等待最重的专家计算而空闲，造成推理效率低下。现有的负载均衡方法主要依赖层内的专家复制或迁移，带来了额外开销并限制了灵活性和可扩展性。

Method: EasyBalance 提出跨层负载均衡策略。核心洞察包括：(1) 其他层的专家对当前层而言可以视为天然冗余；(2) 跨层 MoE 工作负载可以联合执行以缓解各自的不均衡。基于此，EasyBalance 在每个 MoE 步骤中贪心调度一部分跨层工作负载，并将剩余工作负载推迟到未来的均衡机会中执行，从而有效利用跨层不均衡缓解。

Result: 跨模型、任务和配置的大量实验表明，EasyBalance 能持续加速分布式 MoE 推理，将 GPU 空闲时间减少大部分情况下超过 40%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: EasyBalance 通过跨层调度而非依赖专家复制或迁移来实现负载均衡，为分布式 MoE 推理提供了一种零额外开销、即时适应且可扩展的解决方案，显著降低了 GPU 空闲带来的效率损失。

Abstract: Load Balancing has emerged as a critical problem in expert-parallel distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. As routing distributions are typically skewed across experts, devices hosting lighter-loaded experts must idle to wait for the heaviest during expert computing, leading to inefficiency. Existing load-balancing approaches primarily rely on expert replication or migration within each layer, which introduce additional overhead and limit their flexibility and scalability. To address this problem, we propose EasyBalance, a cross-layer load balancing strategy that requires no modifications to the expert-device mapping, enabling instant adaptability and incurring essentially no additional overhead. Our key insights are that (1) experts of other layers can be viewed as naturally redundant for the current layer, and (2) cross-layer MoE workloads can be jointly executed to mitigate their individual imbalance. Based on these observations, EasyBalance greedily schedules a subset of cross-layer workloads to run at each MoE step and defers the remaining workloads for future balancing opportunities, effectively leveraging cross-layer imbalance mitigation. Extensive experiments across models, tasks, and configurations demonstrate that EasyBalance consistently accelerates distributed MoE inference, reducing GPU idling by mostly over 40%. Code is available at https://github.com/yize-wu/EasyInfra.

</details>


### [141] [ZeroLock: Concurrent Memory-Efficient LLM Training via Modular Update Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07974)
*Wentao Dai,Xuanran Li,Yuxiang Zhang,Ming Tang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于边缘端大模型微调的无反向传播（BP-free）算法ZeroLock，通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的chunk更新，打破BP的更新锁定，在收敛率仅相差poly-log因子的前提下，实现了内存降低26.5%、吞吐提升4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端大模型微调面临内存和算力限制，现有流水线并行方案依赖BP训练，而BP存在更新锁定（update locking）这一固有缺陷，导致严重的吞吐和内存瓶颈。因此需要一种无BP的训练算法来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出ZeroLock算法，通过局部目标构造将模型参数更新解耦为多个独立的chunk更新，避免依赖全局反向传播的梯度传播链，从而打破更新锁定的限制。同时建立了首个针对此类局部目标构造方法在一般模型chunk划分下的理论框架，将局部目标与全局目标建立映射关系，并证明了ZeroLock的收敛率为O(1/sqrt(T))，与BP仅相差polylog因子。此外还设计了包含early forwarding和failure recovery等技术的完整系统实现。

Result: 在真实原型上的实验表明，与基于BP的基线相比，ZeroLock将内存占用降低了26.5%，吞吐量提升了4.9%。

Conclusion: ZeroLock通过突破BP的更新锁定限制，在算法层面提升了吞吐并降低了内存消耗，同时保持了与BP相近的收敛性能（仅差polylog因子），为边缘端大模型微调提供了一种高效且理论可保证的BP-free方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning at the edge adapts the model to scenario-specific data while preserving privacy. Although existing studies proposed pipeline parallelism to address the limited memory and computing resources of edge devices, they commonly rely on backpropagation (BP) training, which has a fundamental limitation of update locking and could experience severe throughput and memory bottlenecks. In this work, we propose a BP-free algorithm, called ZeroLock, that decouples the model updates into independent chunk updates by local objective construction. It breaks the update locking of BP and hence can improve throughput at the algorithm level and lower memory usage by reducing activation storage. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first theoretical framework for such local objective construction-based approaches under general model chunk division by mapping local objectives to the global objective. We prove that ZeroLock has a convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$, which differs from BP only by polylogarithmic factors. We design a system for ZeroLock and build real-world prototypes, incorporating techniques such as early forwarding and failure recovery for efficient and robust implementation. Experiments on the prototype show that compared to BP-based baselines, ZeroLock reduces the memory by 26.5% and improves throughput by 4.9%.

</details>


### [142] [Evaluator Ensembles Under Reward Hacking: Covariance Geometry and Finite-Search Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08002)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过协方差几何刻画了语言模型评判器/奖励模型在有限优化下的系统性误差（公共模态误差），证明该误差无法仅从内部评判分数中识别，并提出针对有限搜索误差和遗憾的边界证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型评委和奖励模型虽可实现可扩展监督，但有限优化可能利用评估器错误而非提升响应质量，即优化目标与真实质量脱节的问题需要被系统刻画和量化。

Method: 利用评估器集成的协方差几何：校准情况下，集成均值沿全一方向保留公共模态误差，而评委间分歧仅捕获正交误差。在联合亚高斯模型下，推导best-of-K选择高估和遗憾的界限，并扩展到条件校准下的可预测自适应搜索。提出有界双锚Bernstein证书用于有限搜索误差与遗憾，用高斯应力测试和真实模型审计验证。

Result: 搜索项随log K的平方根缩放且在Gaussian投影误差下渐近紧致；噪声质量代理引入秩一协方差但不改变分歧；120个(J, rho, K)配置的固定种子高斯压力测试及真实模型审计验证了理论，并揭示搜索压力增大时基于分歧的诊断方法的局限。

Conclusion: 分歧不能可靠地作为评估器质量的诊断指标，公共模态误差不可识别意味着需要外部锚定或新的校准方案来缓解有限优化下的评估器剥削问题。

Abstract: Language-model judges and reward models enable scalable supervision, but finite optimization can exploit evaluator errors rather than improve response quality. We characterize this failure through the covariance geometry of evaluator ensembles. For calibrated judges, the ensemble mean retains common-mode error along the all-ones direction, whereas cross-judge disagreement captures only orthogonal error. Consequently, disagreement can be high despite robust aggregation, or low while shared response-dependent errors persist. We prove that common-mode error is not identifiable from internal judge scores alone. Under a joint sub-Gaussian model, we bound best-of-K selection overstatement and target-quality regret, extending the guarantees to predictably adaptive search under conditional calibration. The resulting search terms scale as the square root of log K and are asymptotically tight for Gaussian projected errors. We further show that noisy quality proxies introduce artificial rank-one covariance without changing disagreement, and propose a bounded two-anchor Bernstein certificate for finite-search error and regret. Fixed-seed Gaussian stress tests over 120 (J, rho, K) configurations and real-model audits validate the theory while revealing the limits of disagreement-based diagnostics under increasing search pressure.

</details>


### [143] [Quality-Diversity Stress Tests for Process Reward Models:What Archive Coverage Can and Cannot Certify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08008)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用MAP-Elites质量-多样性搜索对过程奖励模型（PRM）进行压力测试，发现其存在可利用的漏洞（如padding可导致44个严格攻击），并提出配对的LoRA修复协议降低攻击率、提升排序性能，同时不损害最佳回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM对中间推理步骤打分，广泛应用于搜索、排序和训练，但优化过程可能通过增加奖励值的同时将正确推理变成错误推理来利用这些学习到的代理指标。现有研究缺乏系统化的方法来发现和量化这一类正确性翻转（correctness-flipping）漏洞，更缺乏评估修复策略有效性的框架。

Method: 本文将PRM压力测试形式化为一个质量-多样性（QD）搜索问题，采用MAP-Elites算法在行为空间各区域中保留最严重的正确性翻转编辑，同时分离搜索覆盖率与利用覆盖率。该方法通过有限单元修复界限约束覆盖单元的尾部风险与平均残余严重性，并在Lipschitz连续后修复损失与度量覆盖审计条件下，将残余界限分解为存档拟合误差加Lipschitz常数乘以覆盖半径。在真实PRM上，通过受控景观验证证书及分数仅无法提供最坏情况保证的不可可能性结论，并采用预声明的配对LoRA修复协议进行验证。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B上发现聚合依赖的漏洞：padding在均值池化下产生44个严格攻击（最大增益0.294），而最小读出下仅1个攻击；RLHFlow价值头模型呈现相同定性效应（最大增益0.005）。配对LoRA修复协议将攻击率从0.148降至0.037再降至0.074，最坏攻击从0.333降至0.177再至0.212，提升排序AUROC且不降低best-of-4准确率。独立非配对复现确认了结果（44对1，干净分割最坏增益0.0092，MATH-500 41对0，干净排序40/40）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了PRM压力测试与修复的系统性框架：MAP-Elites存档能从理论上保证有限单元修复的残余风险界限，但分数单独无法提供最坏情况的保证。PRM存在真实的聚合方式相关的漏洞，配对LoRA修复是有效的缓解手段，其增益归因于对抗性微调而非存档多样性。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) score intermediate reasoning steps and are widely used for search, ranking, and training, but optimization can exploit these learned proxies by increasing reward while turning correct reasoning into incorrect reasoning. We formulate PRM stress testing as a quality-diversity search problem using MAP-Elites, retaining the most severe correctness-flipping edit in each behavior-space region while separating search coverage from exploit coverage. We characterize what such archives certify: finite-cell repair bounds covered-cell tail risk and average residual severity but cannot bound the worst remaining cell from covered fraction alone; under Lipschitz post-repair loss and metric-cover auditing, the residual is bounded by archive fitting error plus the Lipschitz constant times the covering radius. A controlled landscape validates this certificate and the impossibility of any fraction-only worst-case guarantee. On real PRMs, the search reveals an aggregation-dependent vulnerability in Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B: padding yields 44 strict exploits with maximum gain 0.294 under mean pooling versus one exploit under minimum readout; a matched syntactic control isolates the mechanism, and an RLHFlow value-head model shows the same qualitative effect with maximum gain 0.005. A predeclared paired LoRA repair protocol reduces exploit rates from 0.148 to 0.037 to 0.074, lowers the worst attack from 0.333 to 0.177 to 0.212, improves ranking AUROC without degrading best-of-4 accuracy, attributes gains to adversarial fine-tuning rather than archive diversity, and is confirmed by independent unpaired replications (44 to 1, clean-split worst gain 0.0092, MATH-500 41 to 0, clean ranking 40/40).

</details>


### [144] [Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization for Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08010)
*Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zeen Song,Changwen Zheng,Fanjiang Xu,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R)，用于解决时间序列基础模型在强化学习后训练中出现的"次优坍缩"现象——模型输出分布逐渐偏离真实值的问题。GTN-R以真实值为参考定位高质量区域，引导模型概率质量向真实值邻域靠拢，提高高质量轨迹采样概率，从而缓解次优坍缩并提升预测性能，且可灵活集成到多种RL方法中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在大规模预训练后展现出强泛化能力，强化学习（RL）后训练被广泛用于进一步提升其在下游任务上的性能。然而作者发现RL后训练在部分预测区域可能使模型输出分布逐渐偏离真实值（称为"次优坍缩"），限制了模型性能。分析表明，初始阶段难以在真实值附近采样到高质量轨迹是导致次优坍缩的重要诱因。

Method: 提出Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R)方法：以真实值为参考定位高质量区域，通过正则化项引导模型概率质量向真实值邻域聚集，从而提升高质量轨迹的采样概率。该方法具有通用性，可灵活嵌入不同的RL算法中用于时间序列基础模型的训练。

Result: 大量实验验证了GTN-R的有效性，能够缓解次优坍缩问题并提升时间序列预测性能，同时兼容多种RL方法。

Conclusion: GTN-R通过显式利用真实值信息引导RL后训练过程中的概率分布，有效解决了时间序列基础模型在RL后训练中出现的次优坍缩问题，为提高TSFMs的下游预测性能提供了一种通用、有效的正则化策略。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) plays an important role in a wide range of real-world applications. Recently, time series foundation models (TSFMs), pretrained on large-scale datasets, have demonstrated strong generalization capabilities and emerged as an important paradigm for TSF. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has consequently attracted growing attention as a means of further improving their performance on downstream tasks. However, we find that, in certain forecast regions, RL post-training may gradually shift the output distributions of TSFMs away from the ground truth, thereby limiting their performance. We refer to this phenomenon as \textbf{suboptimal collapse}. Our analysis suggests that difficulty in initially sampling high-quality trajectories near the ground truth is an important contributing factor to suboptimal collapse. To address this issue, we propose Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization (GTN-R) for RL post-training of TSFMs. GTN-R uses the ground truth as a reference for locating high-quality regions and guides the model's probability mass toward the ground-truth neighborhood. This increases the probability of sampling high-quality trajectories, mitigates suboptimal collapse, and improves performance. Moreover, GTN-R can be flexibly integrated into various RL methods for TSFMs. Extensive experiments show its effectiveness.

</details>


### [145] [Do All LLMs Know When They're Being Harmful? A Reproducibility Study of Latent-Space Safety Probes Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08029)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Dun Li Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了Khatri等人提出的轻量级MLP探针方法——在8B模型（LLaMA-3.1-8B）的最后层激活上训练单个探针即可检测有害提示，性能与1000倍大的防护模型相当。作者跨模型家族（Gemma、Mistral、Qwen）验证了该方法的泛化性，并研究了推理中的非确定性影响，发现探针架构可迁移且F1分数接近原始结果，同时发现最终token潜向量在不同随机种子下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始研究证明轻量级MLP探针在单个模型上表现优异，但存在两个未解答的问题：一是该方法能否泛化到其他模型架构和规模，二是推理过程中的非确定性（随机种子）对报告性能有多大影响。回答这些问题对评估该方法的稳健性和实用性至关重要。

Method: 作者端到端复现了原始MLP探针流水线，并在两个方向扩展：(1) 在Gemma-4-E4B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Qwen2-7B等不同架构和规模的模型上训练相同结构的探针，使用WildJailbreak、BeaverTails、AEGIS 2.0三个基准；(2) 通过5个随机种子重复提取激活，测量F1分数的方差以评估非确定性的影响。

Result: 复现LLaMA模型基准时，F1分数与原始结果差距在0.37个百分点以内（BeaverTails上差距小于0.2个百分点）。探针架构成功扩展到其他模型家族，F1分数与LLaMA-3.1-8B报告值差距在一个百分点内。种子变化实验发现：所有测试架构的最终token潜向量在不同种子下保持一致。

Conclusion: 轻量级MLP探针检测有害提示的方法具有良好的跨架构泛化性和可复现性，且推理过程中的随机种子对最终token激活影响极小，表明该方法在实际部署中具有较高的稳定性和可靠性。

Abstract: Khatri et al. (2026) [DOI: 10.1109/DSN-W70714.2026.00027] show that lightweight MLP probes on final-layer activations of a single 8B model (LLaMA-3.1-8B) detect harmful prompts at F1 competitive with guard models 1000x larger, using one probe per benchmark. We reproduce this pipeline end-to-end and extend it along two axes the original study leaves open. First, we test whether the result generalizes across other model architecture and scale by training identical probes on activations from models like Gemma-4-E4B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Qwen2-7B, using the three benchmarks (WildJailbreak, BeaverTails, AEGIS 2.0). Second, we test how much of the reported performance is affected by non-determinism during inference by repeating extraction under five random seeds and measuring the variance of F1 scores. Our results reproduce the original LLaMA model benchmarks within 0.37 percentage points of the original F1 scores (and within 0.2 points on BeaverTails). We find that the original MLP probe architecture extends to other model families with F1 scores within a point of the values reported for LLaMA-3.1-8B. Our experiments varying seed values reveal an interesting observation: final token latent vectors remained the same for all tested architectures irrespective of the seed values used.

</details>


### [146] [CLAM: Causal Spatial Disaggregation to Infer Local Effects From Coarse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08064)
*Gerrit Großmann,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAM方法，从粗粒度观测数据中估计局部因果效应，通过联合学习因果机制和分解映射，利用高分辨率上下文协变量捕捉被聚合数据掩盖的空间异质性因果效应，适用于公共卫生和环境政策等领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从粗分辨率数据中学习细粒度空间模式具有挑战性，尤其是在因果场景中，高分辨率效应必须从聚合的干预和结果中推断。现有方法将问题分离处理时可能遗漏交互信息。

Method: CLAM通过联合学习因果机制与分解映射（disaggregation mapping），利用高分辨率上下文协变量调节因果效应，从而从粗粒度观测中估计局部因果效应。

Result: CLAM支持局部效应估计、反事实推理和原则性的结果分解，在多种设置下可靠地捕捉空间变化的因果效应。

Conclusion: 该方法对公共卫生和环境政策等领域尤为重要，因为这些领域常在宏观尺度上做决策，但存在显著的局部异质性，CLAM为此类场景提供了有效的因果推断工具。

Abstract: Learning fine-grained spatial patterns from coarse-resolution data is challenging, especially in causal settings where high-resolution effects must be inferred from aggregated interventions and outcomes. We introduce CLAM, a method for estimating localized causal effects from coarse observations by exploiting high-resolution contextual covariates that modulate these effects. By jointly learning the causal mechanism and a disaggregation mapping, CLAM captures interactions that are missed when addressing these problems independently. The method supports localized effect estimation, counterfactual reasoning, and principled outcome disaggregation, and reliably captures spatially varying causal effects across diverse settings. This is particularly relevant for applications such as public health and environmental policy, where decisions are made at broad scales despite substantial local heterogeneity. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/clam

</details>


### [147] [Adaptive Symmetry Discovery for Dynamical System Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08091)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究动力系统辨识中的自适应对称性发现，证明当系统具有已知对称性时，可从显著更短的单条轨迹中辨识系统参数，并提出了从单条轨迹自动学习对称群并融入辨识流程的方法，达到与已知对称情形相同的最优轨迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统辨识是科学领域（如生物学、物理学）的核心问题，但现有方法通常假设对称性是已知的。然而实际物理系统往往具有由物理定律强加的对称性，且这些对称性通常是未知的。如何在未知对称群的情况下高效地从单条轨迹中辨识系统参数，是一个尚未解决的关键问题。

Method: 先证明在已知对称性条件下，单条轨迹辨识所需长度显著缩短并给出精确刻画；然后提出自适应对称性发现方法，直接从单条轨迹学习对称群并将其融入辨识过程。理论分析依赖于群表示论工具和Cayley图的扩张性质。

Result: 在已知对称条件下精确刻画了轨迹长度缩短的幅度；提出的自动对称发现方法达到了与已知对称情形相同的理论最优轨迹长度，同时实现了从单条轨迹中自动发现未知对称群。

Conclusion: 对称性信息能够显著提升动力系统辨识的效率，且该优势可以通过自适应对称性学习方法在未知对称群场景下完整保留；所发展的群表示论与Cayley图扩张工具也为动力系统对称性研究提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Dynamical systems model trajectory data generated by fixed underlying dynamics, with applications ranging from biology to physics. Especially in scientific settings, dynamical systems are not generic but often exhibit symmetries imposed by physical laws, formalized through equivariance with respect to group actions. The identification problem concerns recovering the parameters of a system from observed trajectories. In this work, we study adaptive symmetry discovery for dynamical system identification and address how a system can be identified from a single trajectory when it is equivariant with respect to an unknown symmetry group. To this end, we first show that for known symmetries, the system can be identified from a significantly shorter single trajectory than in the generic setting, and we precisely characterize this improvement. We then consider the automatic symmetry discovery setting, proposing a method to learn the symmetry group directly from a single trajectory and incorporate it into the identification procedure, achieving the same optimal trajectory length as in the known-symmetry case. Our analysis relies on tools from group representation theory and the expander properties of Cayley graphs, and may be of independent interest for the study of symmetries in dynamical systems.

</details>


### [148] [Support Selection Beyond Smooth DAG Exactness: Completion Geometry,Score Margins, and Selective Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08103)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从光滑精确性(smooth exactness)出发，系统分析DAG边界上的结构学习可辨识性问题。在DAG边界处，最小环补全生成一个包含精确表示所有受限Taylor jets的平方自由单项式理想；最小补全有q条边时，向量残差的首个可能响应阶为q，非负标量为2q。NOTEARS和DAGMA在远离边界处存在指数多个无排名的等尺度循环流形。对孤立环推导出精确选择时间：当Ψ'(h)≍h^ν时，仅可行性时间为Θ(ε^{-(2ν+1)})；得分边距在ν>0时改变主导动力学，ν=0时出现对数边界层。实验验证该定律，一个无真实值的分离统计量在320条官方NOTEARS/DAGMA轨迹上预测选择时间(Spearman -0.52和-0.66，置换p<10^{-4})。有限样本下，父集置信族和强制相反查询可认证冻结分数的每个群体最优所共享的骨架和无屏蔽碰撞器标签。320次运行中，所有后悔界覆盖独立Oracle分数审计；3,042个认证骨架和2,396个碰撞器标签均与Oracle分数最优一致，但分别有4.4%和5.5%与生成图不一致。这些结果分离了DAG可行性、基于分数的支持选择和因果识别三个层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明特定约束公式存在退化现象，但未隔离光滑精确性本身带来的后果。作者旨在区分DAG可行性、基于分数的支持选择与因果识别这三类不同的问题层次，并揭示光滑精确性约束下结构学习算法的固有局限。

Method: 理论分析上，利用平方自由单项式理想和Taylor jets刻画DAG边界的局部几何；对NOTEARS和DAGMA分析其远离边界的等尺度循环流形；推导孤立环的选择时间渐近行为；有限样本下提出父集置信族与强制相反查询来认证骨架和无屏蔽碰撞器标签。实证上使用320条官方轨迹验证分离统计量的预测能力。

Result: 最小环补全的边数q决定了首个响应阶(q为向量残差，2q为非负标量)；NOTEARS和DAGMA远离边界存在指数多无排名的流形；精确选择时间服从T_0(ε)=Θ(ε^{-(2ν+1)})定律；分离统计量显著预测320条轨迹上的选择时间；认证的骨架和碰撞器标签与Oracle分数最优完全一致，但与生成图存在4.4%和5.5%的不一致。

Conclusion: 光滑精确性本身在DAG边界产生内在的退化与不可排名性，可行性、支持选择和因果识别是三个本质上可分的问题层次。理论预测的选择时间定律得到实验验证，且有限样本下的认证方法能够可靠地分离出与Oracle分数一致的结构信息，即便这些信息与真实因果图不符。

Abstract: Smooth acyclicity constraints answer whether a weighted support is a DAG, whereas structure learning asks which support change should be made. Existing analyses establish degeneracy for particular constraint formulas but do not isolate what follows from smooth exactness itself. At a DAG boundary, we show that minimal cycle completions generate a squarefree monomial ideal containing every restricted Taylor jet of an exact representation. If the smallest completion has $q$ edges, the first possible response has order $q$ for a vector residual and $2q$ for a nonnegative scalar. Exponentially many constant-scale cyclic manifolds exhibit the same lack of ranking away from the boundary for NOTEARS and DAGMA. We derive the exact selection time for an isolated cycle. When $Ψ'(h)\asymp h^ν$, the feasibility-only time is $T_0(\varepsilon)=Θ(\varepsilon^{-(2ν+1)})$; a score margin changes the leading dynamics at scale $T_0^{-1}$ for $ν>0$, while $ν=0$ has a logarithmic boundary layer requiring $γT_0\log(1/\varepsilon)\to0$. Experiments verify this law, and a truth-free separation statistic predicts selection time on 320 official NOTEARS/DAGMA trajectories (Spearman $-0.52$ and $-0.66$, permutation $p<10^{-4}$). For finite samples, a parent-set confidence family and forced-opposite queries certify skeleton and unshielded-collider labels shared by every population optimum of a frozen score. Across 320 runs, every regret bound covers an independent oracle-score audit. None of 3,042 certified skeleton or 2,396 collider labels disagrees with the oracle-score optimum, although 4.4% and 5.5%, respectively, disagree with the generating graph. These results separate DAG feasibility, score-based support selection, and causal identification.

</details>


### [149] [TSDS-Toolbox: A Toolbox for Measuring Time-Series Dataset Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08119)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TSDS-Toolbox，一个用于时间序列数据集相似性度量方法基准测试的统一框架，支持系统性比较、灵活扩展和一致评估，并通过多种实验设置验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列数据集相似性度量方法的实现分散且难以扩展，缺乏统一的基准测试框架，这阻碍了相似性方法在基础模型微调等下游任务中的选择和评估。

Method: 构建TSDS-Toolbox统一框架，提供：(1)系统化、可复现的相似性方法比较；(2)灵活扩展以支持自定义数据集、相似性方法和下游时间序列任务；(3)通过集成的时间序列数据集归约器(reducer)，实现数据集级和序列级相似性方法的一致评估。

Result: 在多种实验设置下进行了全面实验，验证了TSDS-Toolbox的有效性，且工具箱已公开可用。

Conclusion: TSDS-Toolbox为解决时间序列数据集相似性基准测试碎片化问题提供了统一解决方案，有助于研究者更高效地选择源数据集用于基础模型微调，推动时间序列分析研究。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) has significantly accelerated research in time-series analysis, particularly in forecasting, classification, and generation tasks. Recent models, especially foundation models, benefit from time-series dataset similarity due to its significant role in source dataset selection for fine-tuning. However, many existing implementations for benchmarking time-series dataset similarity methods are fragmented and difficult to extend. To address this, we present a unified framework, the Time-Series Dataset Similarity Toolbox (TSDS-Toolbox). Our work enables (1) systematic and reproducible comparisons of time-series dataset similarity methods; (2) flexible extensibility for users to add customized datasets, similarity methods, and downstream time-series tasks; and (3) consistent evaluation of both dataset-level and series-level similarity methods through integrated time-series dataset reducers. The effectiveness of TSDS-Toolbox is validated through comprehensive experiments under diverse experimental settings. Our toolbox is publicly available.

</details>


### [150] [Accurate Ensembles, Fragile Narratives: Multi-Scale Stacking and a Fidelity Audit of LLM-Generated Explanations for Credit Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08126)
*Gregorius Reynaldi Pratama,Kuo-Kun Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个端到端的可解释信贷评分系统（多尺度堆叠集成模型+LLM生成解释），发现排名性能提升真实但操作收益很小（成本加权损失降幅不足2%），而LLM叙事层存在根本性失败（引用与属性不一致的归因、遗漏主导因子、引入未提供的特征），提示即使约束提示词，事后验证而非假设grounding是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信贷评分越来越多地依赖参数无法直接解读的模型，这与监管对不利决策需可解释的要求存在张力。常见的解决方案是用语言模型将特征归因转化为自然语言解释，但该方案的第二半（LLM叙事层）是否真的成立缺乏系统验证。本文旨在端到端构建该系统并实证检验其承诺是否兑现。

Method: 预测组件采用多尺度堆叠集成：四种不同正则化的梯度提升学习器与残差网络融合，通过基于袋外预测训练的神经元学习器整合。在包含32,581条申请的公开信贷数据集上评估。叙事组件将SHAP/LIME归因交给LLM生成解释，并对归因的一致性与可解释性进行量化测量（overlap@10、tau秩相关、符号一致性、校准度ECS、扰动稳定性DPD）。

Result: 集成模型测试ROC-AUC达0.9539，PR-AUC 0.9137，显著优于最佳单模型（Delta-AUC=0.0143，p=0.016）。但在F1最优阈值下仅额外避免6个未检出的违约（共1,422个），成本加权损失降幅不足2%。叙事层方面，SHAP与LIME在特征重要性重叠度上较高（overlap@10=0.80）但排序一致性低（tau=0.43），归因符号对最敏感输入近乎随机（模态符号份额0.53）。LLM在审计案例中引用与属性相反的归因、遗漏主导驱动因子并引入未提供的特征。

Conclusion: 研究结论是不对称的：集成模型带来的排名提升真实但操作上收益有限；LLM叙事层存在根本性缺陷，单纯提示工程无法修复。grounding必须在生成后验证而非默认成立，这是构建可部署的可解释信贷评分系统的关键要求。

Abstract: Credit scoring increasingly relies on models whose decision logic cannot be read off their parameters, in tension with supervisory expectations that adverse decisions be explainable. A common proposal closes that gap with a language model: compute feature attributions, hand them to an LLM, and let it write the rationale. We build such a system end to end and test whether the second half of the promise holds. The predictive component is a multi-scale stacking ensemble fusing four differently regularised gradient-boosting learners with a residual network through a neural meta-learner trained on out-of-fold predictions. On a public 32,581-application credit dataset it reaches test ROC-AUC 0.9539 (95% CI [0.9462, 0.9616]) and PR-AUC 0.9137, beating the best single model by Delta-AUC = 0.0143 (p = 0.016 under a conservative independence assumption). Our central finding is asymmetric. The ranking gain is real but operationally small: at the F1-optimal threshold the ensemble avoids only six additional missed defaults out of 1,422 against a tuned random forest, cutting cost-weighted loss by under 2%. The narrative layer fails in a way prompt engineering alone does not fix. In an audited case the model named three factors as risk-increasing that the supplied attributions scored as risk-reducing, omitted the dominant driver, and introduced a feature never given to it. We trace this to properties we measure rather than assume: SHAP and LIME agree on which features matter (overlap@10 = 0.80) but not on their order (tau = 0.43, p = 0.18), and the attribution sign for the model's most sensitive input is near a coin flip across applicants (modal-sign share 0.53). Calibration (ECS = 0.117) and perturbation stability (DPD = 0.078) both fall short of our own thresholds. Constrained prompting is necessary but not sufficient: grounding must be verified after generation, not assumed.

</details>


### [151] [DoGMA: A Central-Dogma-Guided Foundation Model for Multi-Omics Alignment and Multi-Task Learning in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08148)
*Junfei Ling,Bangzheng Pu,Bingsen Xue,Tianle Li,Ruying Hu,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DoGMA，一个以中心法则为指导的泛癌多组学基础模型，通过Transformer-MoE架构中的定向注意力偏差和掩码分层组学重建预训练，实现符合生物学信息流动方向的多组学交互，在多种下游任务中表现出强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制在多组学模型中允许无限制的双向交互，但忽略了中心法则所揭示的生命信息流动方向性。这种忽视可能导致模型在异质性癌症、下游任务和不完整模态场景中的迁移能力受限。

Method: 基于Transformer-MoE架构构建，通过定向注意力机制将组学间通信约束为遵循中心法则的信息流方向；采用掩码分层组学重建作为预训练任务，引导模型学习与中心法则一致的组学交互模式。

Result: 在癌症表征学习、生存预测和转移预测等多个下游任务上展现出持续的强预测性能；消融实验和分析表明性能提升源于中心法则引导的定向注意力与重建预训练的协同作用。

Conclusion: DoGMA验证了领域特定归纳偏置能够提升多组学基础模型的鲁棒性和可迁移性，为多组学表征学习中注意力机制的设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms have been widely utilized in modern deep learning, and many existing multi-omics models inherit their conventional use to allow unrestricted bidirectional interactions. However, the fundamental logic of life is directional. Existing designs often overlook the directionality suggested by the central dogma, potentially limiting transfer across heterogeneous cancers, downstream tasks, and incomplete modality settings.In this work, we present DoGMA, a central-dogma-guided foundation model for pan-cancer multi-omics analysis, arguing that robust transfer requires representations with domain-specific inductive bias. Concretely, we build it on a Transformer-MoE architecture where directed attention biases inter-omics communication toward central-dogma information flow. We further pretrain our model with masked hierarchical omics reconstruction to guide it toward learning central-dogma-consistent interactions. Across diverse downstream tasks, including cancer representation learning, survival prediction, and metastasis prediction, DoGMA consistently demonstrates strong predictive performance. Ablations and analyses further suggest that the performance gains arise from the synergy between central-dogma-guided directed attention and reconstruction-based pretraining, which together promote more biologically consistent cross-omics information exchange. Overall, DoGMA demonstrates that domain-specific inductive biases can improve the robustness and transferability of multi-omics foundation models, offering new insights into the design of attention mechanisms for multi-omics representation learning.

</details>


### [152] [A Hybrid Nested Harness for Decoupling Structure and Parameters in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08156)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合嵌套搜索框架，将LLM作为外部循环提出带数值间隙的结构草图，内部数值优化器（如CMA-ES、梯度优化或MCMC采样器）对间隙进行微调，解决了LLM在连续参数优化上效率低下的问题，并在三个科学领域验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的进化算法中，LLM作为单一算子同时更新结构组件和连续参数，但LLM擅长前者而不擅长后者，在离散跳跃和试错循环中浪费token。需要一种方法将结构探索与数值优化分离，各司其职。

Method: 提出混合嵌套搜索框架：外循环由LLM提出带数值间隙的结构草图，内循环由数值优化器（CMA-ES、梯度方法或MCMC采样器）对间隙进行数值调优。内外求解器均可插拔，支持文本优化器与零阶、梯度或贝叶斯采样的任意组合。

Result: 在三个科学领域（闭式测试函数的元优化器、系统研究与社会困境的基于代码的策略、近似贝叶斯推断任务）中，混合优化器均优于纯LLM驱动的搜索和纯数值优化基线。

Conclusion: 将LLM的结构生成能力与数值优化器的精细调参能力分离组合，能在各类科学优化任务中取得比单一方法更好的性能，且框架具有高度的模块化和可扩展性。

Abstract: In evolutionary algorithms powered by language models, the LLM acts as a single operator that simultaneously updates structural components (like control flow) and continuous parameters. While LLMs can be good at the first, they are not efficient at the second, wasting tokens taking discrete jumps inside a trial and error loop. We resolve this by formalizing a hybrid nested search, in which an outer loop has the LLM propose a structural sketch, with numeric gaps, and an inner numerical optimizer tunes the sketch. Both the outer and inner solvers are pluggable: any text-based optimizer can be combined with a zero-order optimizer (CMA-ES), gradient-based routines, or MCMC samplers. We validate our framework across three scientific domains: (i) meta-optimizers on closed-form test functions, (ii) code-based policies for systems research and social dilemmas; and (iii) approximate Bayesian inference tasks. Across all three, the hybrid optimizer is superior to both vanilla LLM-driven search and pure numerical optimization baselines. Code at: https://github.com/vicgalle/hybrid-nested-search

</details>


### [153] [Predicting blood clot growth from sparse post-onset measurements with latent neural differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08165)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Ying Qian,He Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于隐式神经微分方程的計算框架，可从稀疏测量数据推断凝血模型未知参数并预测血栓进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算凝血模型有望加深对血栓形成的理解，但其临床转化受限于许多模型输入难以测量且患者特异性数据稀疏的问题。

Method: 采用隐式神经微分方程框架，结合多物理场凝血模型（凝血级联与扩散主导血凝块生长），利用四种已知生化输入（纤维蛋白原及因子IX、VIII、V）和稀疏早期血凝块尺寸观测推断组织因子参数，并比较七种概率方法（SNODE、SNFDE、隐式神经过程基线、单调概率深度集成、经验轨迹检索、PCA-岭高斯后验及Gompertz曲线检索）的性能。

Result: SNODE在未知输入推断和未来血凝块生长轨迹预测方面表现最佳；SNFDE表现相近且优于其他非微分模型。随着可用观测增多预测精度提高，而预测时域延长则增加不确定性并降低精度。

Conclusion: 隐式神经微分方程能够有效结合参数推断与血凝块生长预测，为个性化血栓建模提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Computational models of blood clotting improve understanding of thrombus formation, but their clinical application remains limited because many model inputs are difficult to measure and patient-specific data are often sparse. We present a computational framework based on latent neural differential equations that infers unknown model parameters from sparse measurements and forecasts thrombosis progression. We demonstrate the framework using data generated from a multiphysics blood-clotting model in which clot growth is governed by the coagulation cascade and diffusion. Four known biochemical inputs (fibrinogen and factors IX, VIII, and V), together with sparse early clot-size observations, are used to infer the tissue-factor parameter and predict subsequent clot growth. We compare seven probabilistic methods: stochastic neural ordinary differential equations (SNODE), stochastic neural functional differential equations (SNFDE), a latent neural-process baseline, a monotone probabilistic deep ensemble, empirical trajectory retrieval, PCA-ridge Gaussian posterior, and Gompertz-curve retrieval. SNODE achieved the best performance in inferring the unknown input and forecasting future clot-growth trajectories. SNFDE performed similarly and consistently outperformed the other non-differential models. Prediction accuracy improved as more observations became available, whereas longer forecasting horizons increased uncertainty and decreased accuracy. Latent neural differential equations thus effectively combine parameter inference and clot-growth forecasting from sparse measurements, providing a promising foundation for personalized thrombosis modeling.

</details>


### [154] [Biologically Informed Representation Learning for Robust Cross-Center Generalization of MALDI-TOF Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08182)
*Alejandro L. García-Navarro,Carlos Sevilla-Salcedo,Belén Rodríguez-Sánchez,Vanessa Gómez-Verdejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DALMA框架，一种概率表示学习方法，结合领域特异性重建和生物学监督，为MALDI-TOF质谱的临床微生物学任务（如微生物鉴定和抗菌素耐药性预测）学习跨中心可转移的潜在表示，实现零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在MALDI-TOF质谱的临床微生物学任务中表现出潜力，但跨机构的域偏移导致模型捕获技术伪影而非可转移的生物学信息。现有表示学习方法主要依赖统计域对齐，忽视了微生物学数据集中天然的生物学监督信息。

Method: DALMA是一个概率表示学习框架，联合建模采集特异性变异和生物学监督。通过将领域特异性重建与生物学引导的表示学习相结合，学习生物学结构化的潜在表示，推理时不需机构特异性组件，实现零样本跨中心部署。

Result: 在覆盖三个国家七个数据集的多中心基准上，DALMA在两个留出临床中心上持续达到最先进的零样本微生物鉴定性能，学习到的表示也能有效迁移到抗菌素耐药性预测。潜在空间的新颖性估计在未见过的域偏移下实现了可靠的预测性选择。

Conclusion: 生物学信息引导的表示学习为临床微生物学中的稳健可迁移机器学习提供了一种有效策略，能够在不依赖推理时机构特定组件的情况下实现跨临床中心泛化。

Abstract: Machine learning models for MALDI-TOF mass spectrometry have shown considerable promise for clinical microbiology tasks such as microbial identification and antimicrobial resistance prediction. However, their deployment across institutions remains limited by domain shift, as acquisition-specific variability often leads models to capture technical artifacts rather than transferable biological information. Existing representation learning approaches primarily address this problem through statistical domain alignment while largely overlooking the biological supervision naturally available in microbiology datasets. We introduce DALMA, a probabilistic representation learning framework that jointly models acquisition-specific variability and biological supervision to learn biologically structured latent representations. By combining domain-specific reconstruction with biologically guided representation learning, DALMA learns transferable representations that generalize across heterogeneous clinical centers without requiring institution-specific components at inference, enabling zero-shot deployment on previously unseen sites. We evaluate DALMA on a multi-center benchmark comprising seven datasets from three countries. DALMA consistently achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot microbial identification across two held-out clinical centers, while the learned representations also transfer effectively to antimicrobial resistance prediction. Furthermore, latent-space novelty estimation enables reliable selective prediction under previously unseen domain shifts. These results demonstrate that biologically informed representation learning provides an effective strategy for robust and transferable ML in clinical microbiology.

</details>


### [155] [Beyond Aggregate Calibration: Decomposing Income-Conditional Recall Disparities in Automated Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08202)
*Sai Srikar Boddupalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在LendingClub大规模消费贷款数据集（N=1,344,936）上发现：基于模型置信度分数的数据清洗管线对高收入违约者存在系统性误分类（将其误判为标签噪声），造成高收入与低收入违约者之间16.86个百分点的真正例率（召回率）差距。通过顺序特征遮蔽方法隔离出三种偏差机制：对自报收入的直接依赖、通过发起利率吸收的制度性偏差，以及消除收入和利率后的残余差距（交叉验证3.55个百分点，留出测试集2.56个百分点，Z=-4.04, p<0.0001），后者由贷款金额和房屋所有权等结构性代理变量维持。研究表明仅遮蔽敏感属性不足以确保公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心的策展管线广泛依赖模型置信度分数来标记和过滤噪声或错误标注的训练实例，但这种筛选惯例可能引入系统性偏见。本文旨在揭示这一看似中性的技术实践如何在不同收入群体间产生算法歧视，并分析其背后的多重机制。

Method: 在LendingClub的134万条贷款样本上，首先评估基于置信度分数的噪声过滤行为，使用Cramer's V量化人口统计不对称性；然后通过等机会框架（Hardt et al. 2016）衡量真正例率差距；采用顺序特征遮蔽（sequential feature-blinding）方法区分三种偏差机制：直接依赖自报收入、制度性偏差（通过发起利率编码）、以及盲化收入与利率后仍存在的残余差距；最后利用样本外带符号SHAP值分析残余差距的结构性代理变量来源。

Result: 高收入违约者被误判为标签噪声的比例显著高于低收入违约者（Cramer's V约0.03-0.07）；高收入与低收入违约者之间存在16.86个百分点的真正例率（召回率）差距；在顺序移除收入特征和利率特征后，残余差距仍有3.55个百分点（交叉验证）和2.56个百分点（留出测试集，Z=-4.04, p<0.0001）；SHAP分析表明该残余差距主要由贷款金额和房屋所有权状态等结构性代理变量维持。

Conclusion: 仅从模型中移除敏感属性（如收入）并不能实现公平，因为制度性定价决策（利率）和行为代理变量（贷款金额、房屋所有权）会共同重构被省略的信号。研究为受监管金融机构中的数据为中心AI工作流审计提供了实际启示，指出需要超越简单的特征盲化方法，系统性审视数据策展管线中的多重偏差传导路径。

Abstract: Data-centric curation pipelines frequently rely on model confidence scores to flag and filter noisy or mislabeled training instances. Evaluating this filtering convention on a large-scale consumer lending sample (LendingClub, N = 1,344,936) uncovers an underlying demographic asymmetry: high-income defaulters are disproportionately classified as label noise relative to low-income defaulters (Cramer's V approximately 0.03-0.07). Re-examining this behavior through the lens of equal opportunity [Hardt et al., 2016] reveals a far more severe discrepancy: a 16.86 percentage point gap in true positive rate (recall) between high- and low-income borrowers who ultimately defaulted. Implementing a sequential feature-blinding methodology allows us to isolate the drivers of this disparity across three distinct mechanisms: (1) direct reliance on self-reported applicant income; (2) algorithmic absorption of upstream institutional bias encoded within origination interest rates; and (3) a residual disparity (3.55 percentage points in cross-validation; 2.56 percentage points on a held-out test partition, Z = -4.04, p < 0.0001) that remains even after purging both income and interest rates from the model. Out-of-sample signed SHAP valuations demonstrate that this residual gap is maintained by structural proxies, most notably loan amount and home ownership status. These empirical findings show that simply blinding an algorithm to sensitive attributes fails to ensure fairness when institutional pricing decisions and behavioral proxy variables collectively reconstruct the omitted signals. We outline the practical implications of these findings for auditing data-centric AI workflows within regulated financial institutions.

</details>


### [156] [FreSH: Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08207)
*Pingping Liu,Muyao Wang,Zijian Zhang,Tongshun Zhang,Hao Miao,Guorui Xie,Qingliang Li,Qiuzhan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreSH是一个频率分段层次化多专家框架，通过自适应多尺度时间信号分析、局部专业分工与全局上下文建模相结合的方式，在30个UEA基准数据集和真实振动数据上优于现有方法，同时大幅减少模型规模和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列分类需要在多个尺度上有效捕捉复杂时间模式，同时保持计算效率。然而现有方法难以在类别不平衡和现实约束下调和细粒度表示学习，模型精度与效率之间存在矛盾。

Method: FreSH采用频率分段层次化多专家架构：1) 通过频率分段实现自适应多尺度时间信号分析；2) 将局部专业化与全局上下文建模相结合以增强表示能力；3) 设计自适应融合策略动态强调输入中最具信息量的成分；4) 引入更强的鲁棒优化目标，提升在不同样本难度和类别分布下的学习稳定性。

Result: 在30个UEA基准数据集和真实世界振动数据上的广泛评估表明，FreSH在分类准确率上持续优于现有最先进方法，同时显著降低模型大小并提升效率。

Conclusion: FreSH证明了通过频率分段与层次化多专家设计的组合，可以在保持计算效率的同时实现强大的多尺度表示学习能力，为MTSC任务提供了一种兼顾精度与效率的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Classification (MTSC) demands models that can effectively capture complex temporal patterns across multiple scales while remaining computationally efficient. However, existing approaches generally struggle to reconcile fine-grained representation learning, especially under class imbalance and real-world constraints. In this paper, we present FreSH, a Frequency-Segmented Hierarchical Multi-Expert Framework designed to address these challenges. FreSH introduces a new perspective for MTSC by enabling adaptive, multi-scale analysis of temporal signals, allowing different aspects of the data to be modeled in a complementary and coordinated manner. By combining localized specialization with holistic context modeling, FreSH achieves strong representational capacity without incurring excessive computational overhead. An adaptive fusion strategy further enhances flexibility, enabling the model to dynamically emphasize the most informative components of the input. In addition, we incorporate a more robust optimization objective that improves learning stability across varying sample difficulties and class distributions. Extensive evaluations on 30 UEA benchmark datasets and real-world vibration data demonstrate that FreSH consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in classification accuracy, while substantially reducing model size and efficiency.

</details>


### [157] [Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning: Decoupling the Shared Control Bottleneck of RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08224)
*Binwen Tan,Jingchao Wang,Dengzhe Hou,Lingyu Jiang,Zeyuan Wu,Yunhan Shen,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori Yamada,Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"事后训练控制系数"来衡量RL微调中组件对奖励增益的实际控制力（而非仅看激活强度），并以此定义Shared Control Bottleneck和Activation-Control Gap两个诊断指标。基于此提出Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT)正则化方法，通过一阶代理梯度（开销低于8%）在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.2-3B上实现控制解耦与多任务能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL微调的可解释性研究主要归因于"更强且更多样的电路激活"，但激活不等于控制——一个被激活的电路未必控制着训练后的奖励增益。基准分数只告诉模型是否变好，却不揭示内部发生了什么变化，也不说明模型如何在有限能力下跨任务分配资源。作者希望将"激活"与"控制"分离，建立真正的因果归因体系。

Method: 借鉴代谢控制分析（Metabolic Control Analysis），定义Post-training Control Coefficient测量组件对奖励增益的控制力，将其按任务族排列为控制矩阵与激活幅度矩阵。定义Shared Control Bottleneck（跨任务控制集中度）和Activation-Control Gap（激活与控制集中度之差）。当间隙小时，控制塌缩到共享方向并丧失任务特异性。为缓解塌缩，提出CD-RFT：在后训练损失中加入Shared Control Bottleneck正则项。精确正则化梯度需要与flash attention不兼容的二阶自动微分，因此推导出一阶代理梯度，最坏开销低于8%。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，CD-RFT实现了最大的控制解耦，并在数学、代码、逻辑任务上相比匹配的GRPO提升了多任务能力。无KL变体在pass@1上领先，KL惩罚变体在大k的pass@k覆盖率上领先（而KL本身会损害该指标）。控制解耦与能力提升均迁移到Llama-3.2-3B上。

Conclusion: Shared Control Bottleneck既是机制诊断工具也是训练正则化器，证明了"共享激活与任务特化控制可以共存"这一关键见解。这挑战了仅以激活为中心的RL可解释性叙事，为多任务RL后训练提供了新的控制层面的优化方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training unlocks complex reasoning in LLMs. Yet benchmark scores reveal only whether a model improved, not what changed inside it, nor how it splits finite capability across tasks. A representative interpretability line attributes the success of RL fine-tuning to stronger and more diverse circuit activation. We challenge this activation-centered account by separating activation from control: an activated circuit need not control the post-training reward gain. Adapting Metabolic Control Analysis, we define the Post-training Control Coefficient to measure component control over reward gain and arrange these coefficients by task family into a control matrix, paired with an activation-magnitude matrix. We call cross-task control concentration the Shared Control Bottleneck and the difference between activation and control concentration the Activation-Control Gap. This reveals that highly shared activations can coexist with task-specific control, while a small gap indicates that control has collapsed onto a shared direction and lost task specificity. To reduce this collapse, we regularize the post-training loss with the Shared Control Bottleneck and propose Control-Diverse Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CD-RFT). The exact regularizer gradient requires second-order automatic differentiation incompatible with flash attention, so we derive a first-order proxy with worst-case overhead below eight percent. On Qwen2.5-7B, CD-RFT achieves the largest control decoupling and improves multi-task capability over matched GRPO across mathematics, code, and logic. The no-KL variant leads on pass@1, and the KL-penalized variant leads on large-k pass@k coverage that KL otherwise degrades. Together, these results show that the Shared Control Bottleneck is both a mechanistic diagnostic and a training regularizer, and that control decoupling and capability gains transfer to Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [158] [SAGE: SLO-Aware Adaptive Retrieval for Production RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08237)
*Muhammad Faizan Raza,Shuo,Yang,Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种基于学习的SLO感知自适应检索策略，通过轻量特征动态选择每个查询的检索段落数量k，在保持答案质量的同时满足尾部延迟和成本约束。在NQ数据集上，SAGE在5秒P95延迟SLO下达到95%的SLO合规率（静态基线仅30%），同时降低36%的P95延迟和51%的检索成本，仅损失2个百分点EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RAG系统需在尾部延迟SLO和基础设施成本约束下运行，但标准检索管线使用固定检索预算，无法根据查询难度动态调整——简单查询过度检索浪费资源，困难查询检索不足导致质量下降，迫使运维人员在答案质量与SLO合规之间权衡取舍。

Method: SAGE采用模仿学习范式：(1) 从初始检索结果中提取轻量特征（得分分布、排名间隙、词汇信号等）表征查询难度；(2) 离线训练时，由一个近似最优延迟-质量权衡的oracle生成示范数据；(3) 推理时仅基于特征预测每查询应检索的段落数k，无需额外LLM调用，开销极小。

Result: 在Natural Questions上，5秒P95延迟SLO下，SAGE获得95%的SLO合规率（最佳静态基线k=20仅为30%），P95延迟降低36%，检索成本降低51%，EM仅损失2个百分点。在NQ上训练的单一策略可泛化至HotpotQA、UnSeenTimeQA以及四个LLM家族（Llama、Qwen、Mistral、Gemma），持续带来+45-52点的SLO改进且无质量退化。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了学习自适应的按查询检索预算分配是可行且有效的：通过轻量特征和模仿学习，可以在不牺牲答案质量的前提下显著提升SLO合规率并降低检索成本，且策略具有良好的跨数据集和跨模型泛化能力，为生产环境RAG系统的检索预算自适应化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems in production operate under strict service level objectives (SLOs) on tail latency and infrastructure cost. However, standard retrieval pipelines rely on fixed retrieval budgets that ignore query difficulty, over-retrieving for easy queries and under-serving hard ones, forcing operators to trade answer quality against SLO compliance. This paper proposes SAGE, a learned SLO-aware adaptive retrieval policy that dynamically selects the number of passages k per query. SAGE uses lightweight features derived from initial retrieval (e.g., score distributions, rank gaps, lexical signals) and is trained offline via imitation learning from an oracle that approximates optimal latency-quality trade-offs. At inference, it adds no LLM calls and minimal overhead. On Natural Questions, under a 5s P95 latency SLO, SAGE achieves 95% SLO compliance versus 30% for the best static baseline (k=20), reduces P95 latency by 36% and retrieval cost by 51% with only 2 percentage points Exact Match (EM) loss. A single policy trained on Natural Questions generalizes across HotpotQA, UnSeenTimeQA, and four LLM families (Llama, Qwen, Mistral, Gemma), consistently yielding +45-52 point SLO improvements without quality degradation.

</details>


### [159] [The Replay Gap: Static Evaluation of Model Switching in LLM Agents Scores the Wrong World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08239)
*Ashritha Gonuguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分支回放（branching rollouts）实验证明，基于日志拼接（log-stitching）的重放评估方法在智能体（agentic）路由场景下失效：在6组配对实验中，模型交换（swap）导致61-94%的动作被重写，74-77%的早期交换在第一个动作即发生分歧，仅有3%的重放状态有效，且所有结果翻转均只出现在交换组而非对照组。这揭示了重放评估"评估了错误的世界"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由在单轮场景中通过回放日志评估已有成熟方法，但智能体路由（每步动态路由）的评估仍沿用单轮评估范式：回放已记录的轨迹并替换某一模型的输出，假设其余轨迹不受影响。这种假设在智能体场景下可能严重失真，因为模型输出会影响后续所有环境状态和动作序列。

Method: 作者提出"分支回放"（branching rollouts）评估方法：在受控时间点fork实时SWE-bench智能体轨迹，重建环境状态，然后分别用不同模型继续该分支，并与用相同模型继续的对照组（控制采样与重放噪声）进行对比。共进行6组配对运行（约900次rollouts），对比交换组与对照组在归一化编辑距离、动作分歧率和结果翻转上的差异。同时与传统的日志拼接重放评估（log-stitching replay evaluator）进行交叉验证。

Result: 1) 模型交换的差异超过匹配的对照底线+0.25到+0.66归一化编辑距离（多重校正CI排除零）；2) 61-94%的交换后动作被重写，74-77%的早期交换在交换后第一个动作即分歧（对照仅6-35%），仅3%的重放状态有效；3) 分歧度随fork深度增加而降低（双向成立）；4) 所有5个结果翻转都出现在交换组（升级模型挽救未解决样本、降级模型丢失唯一解法），359个对照fork中零翻转；5) 日志拼接重放对所有成功相关结果调用均误判，预测补丁与真实相似度仅0.00-0.11；6) 温度0"确定性"依赖于配置：FP8服务的对照在90%以上fork上分歧，AWQ服务则近乎一致；7) 在严格预算下更强模型反而更常耗尽步数不提交。

Conclusion: 基于重放的基准在智能体路由场景下评估的是一个错误的世界——重放评估无法可靠度量智能体路由的真实性能，因为模型交换导致的轨迹分歧使历史状态失效。研究呼吁智能体路由评估应采用分支回放等实时方法，并发布评估工具链与全部轨迹数据以供社区复现。

Abstract: LLM routers promise efficiency by matching each request to the cheapest adequate model, and are increasingly applied per step inside multi-step agents. Yet agentic routers are evaluated like single-turn routers: by replaying logged trajectories and substituting another model's recorded outputs, assuming the rest of the trajectory is unaffected. We test this assumption with branching rollouts: we fork live SWE-bench agent trajectories at controlled points, rebuild the environment, continue each fork with a different model, and compare against same-model control forks that isolate sampling and replay noise. Across six paired runs (~900 rollouts), swaps exceed their matched control floors by +0.25 to +0.66 normalized edit distance (multiplicity-corrected CIs exclude zero), rewriting 61-94% of post-fork actions; 74-77% of early swaps diverge at the first post-fork action, versus 6-35% of controls, leaving only 3% of replayed states valid. Divergence decreases with fork depth in both directions. All five outcome flips we observe occur in swap arms, upgrades rescuing unsolved instances and a downgrade losing the sole solve, and zero occur across 359 control forks. Scoring these same swaps with a log-stitching replay evaluator, replay mispredicts every success-relevant outcome call and predicts patches with 0.00-0.11 similarity to reality. Auditing the noise floor, temperature-0 "determinism" is configuration-dependent: FP8-served controls diverge on over 90% of forks while AWQ-served ones remain near-identical; and under tight budgets the stronger model more often exhausts its steps without submitting. Replay-based benchmarks score the wrong world for agentic routing; we release our harness and all trajectories.

</details>


### [160] [Learning from Environmental Feedback: Credit Assignment across Multiple Timescales for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08255)
*Yifu Huo,Shunjie Xing,Chenglong Wang,Peinan Feng,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Anxiang Ma,Yuxin Gao,Tongran Liu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EFCA（基于环境反馈的信用分配）方法，利用环境交互中自然生成的多时间尺度过程信号（短期反馈和中期状态历史信号）来解决智能体强化学习中延迟稀疏奖励的信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习常面临延迟且稀疏的奖励问题，信用分配是解决这一挑战的有效途径。然而现有信用分配方法忽略了环境交互过程中自然产生的丰富过程信息（如交互历史），而这些信息能为识别单个动作的贡献提供有价值的监督信号。

Method: 提出EFCA（Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment），一种面向长时程智能体强化学习的多时间尺度信用分配方法。EFCA用两个环境接地（environment-grounded）的过程信号补充长期结果信号：短期反馈信号（捕捉当前动作的即时效应）和中期状态历史信号（识别近期交互中的无效模式）。两个信号都直接从环境反馈中提取，并通过回报重加权机制（return reweighting mechanism）进行整合。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上的实验表明，EFCA在任务成功率和任务质量两方面均一致地优于强基线，验证了环境接地多时间尺度信用分配对长时程智能体强化学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 环境反馈中天然包含的多时间尺度过程信号能够有效增强信用分配，为长时程智能体强化学习中的延迟稀疏奖励问题提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) often suffers from delayed and sparse rewards in real-world environments. A promising solution to this challenge is credit assignment, which aims to decompose trajectory-level rewards and provide more fine-grained supervision for intermediate decisions. However, existing credit assignment approaches ignore the rich process information naturally generated during environment interaction, e.g., interaction history. We argue that such information provides valuable supervision for identifying the contribution of individual actions. To this end, we propose Environmental Feedback-based Credit Assignment (EFCA), a multi-timescale credit assignment approach for long-horizon agentic RL. EFCA complements the long-term outcome signal with two environment-grounded process signals: a short-term feedback signal that captures the immediate effect of the current action and a medium-term state-history signal that identifies ineffective patterns from recent interactions. Both signals are directly extracted from environment feedback and integrated through a return reweighting mechanism. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that EFCA consistently improves both task success and task quality over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of environment-grounded multi-timescale credit assignment for long-horizon agentic RL.

</details>


### [161] [Opportunity Is Not Realizability: Selection-Valid Diagnostics for Multi-LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08265)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"Oracle路由"诊断的两个缺陷（同例验证破坏配对推断、全信息Oracle不可部署），提出将估计目标分离为三种（结果Oracle机会、声明信号下的贝叶斯最优增益、学习路由器的留出增益），并构建了验证选择有效性的置信区间、信号信息夹逼以及子模互补覆盖下的(1-1/e)贪心保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oracle路由用于衡量语言模型池从按查询选择中可获得的增益，但现有诊断存在两个缺陷：在同一批样例上选择最佳固定模型进行测试会使配对推断失效；且全信息Oracle看到的结果是任何可部署路由器都无法观测到的。因此需要更严谨的统计框架来评估路由的真实可部署增益。

Method: 将估计目标分离为三种：结果Oracle机会、来自预声明信号的贝叶斯最优增益、以及学习路由器的留出增益。证明经过选择（选出最佳固定模型或路由器族最佳成员）仍然有效的置信区间；提出信号信息夹逼（signal-information sandwich）；利用子模互补覆盖性质给出构建紧凑池的(1-1/e)贪心保证。

Result: 在6个家族8个检查点、4个基准上：验证选择有效性的区间证明每个任务上群体Oracle差距为9.7-30.7分；而最强可部署提示路由器仅恢复其中7.5-14.4%；对11种测试策略中最佳者的同时置信区间下界为零。强路由器确实优于最佳固定模型，但Oracle差距的大部分仍然无法实现。

Conclusion: 可实现的Oracle机会份额是小的且可被证明：强路由器能击败最佳固定模型，但绝大多数Oracle增益仍然不可实现。该工作为路由器评估提供了统计上有效的方法论，并揭示了当前路由器与理论上限之间的实质性差距。

Abstract: Oracle routing measures how much a pool of language models could gain from per-query selection, but the diagnostic has two flaws: testing against a best fixed model selected on the same examples invalidates paired inference, and a full-information oracle sees outcomes no deployable router observes. We separate three estimands (outcome-oracle opportunity, the Bayes-optimal gain from a declared pre-answer signal, and the held-out gain of a learned router) and prove selection-valid confidence intervals that survive choosing the best fixed model or the best member of a router family, a signal-information sandwich, and a $(1-1/e)$ greedy guarantee for building compact pools from submodular complementary coverage. On eight checkpoints from six families over four benchmarks, selection-valid intervals certify a population oracle gap of $9.7$--$30.7$ points on every task, yet the strongest deployable prompt router recovers only $7.5$--$14.4\%$ of it, and the simultaneous interval for the best of eleven tested policies has lower limit zero throughout. The realizable share of oracle opportunity is small and certifiable: strong routers beat the best fixed model, and most of the gap remains.

</details>


### [162] [Stateful CARS: Exact Cross-History Reuse for Policy-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08282)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究工具使用型语言模型代理在受硬状态验证器约束下的精确采样问题。作者提出stateful CARS方法，在每次尝试中冻结一组声音的"状态-延续"模式，并移除所有在匹配的抽象状态处包含认证延续的轨迹，通过精确残差Doob变换从提议分布中采样。理论证明了模式合理性、自适应精确性、i.i.d.输出、几乎必然终止、单调接受和压缩不变性，并描述了受可达完整历史乘积状态数约束的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型语言模型代理面临随观测和先前动作而变化的状态约束，现有方法难以在硬状态验证器约束下进行精确采样。需要一种能在历史变化条件下重用无效性证书、同时保证采样精确性的方法。

Method: 提出Stateful CARS方法：在每次尝试中冻结一组声音的state-continuation模式库，并移除所有在匹配抽象状态处包含认证延续的轨迹；通过精确残差Doob变换从提议分布中进行精确采样。给出了可检查的未来有效性互模拟条件，用于验证模式的合理性。

Result: 在可枚举工作流上，分析解与有效条件分布匹配至10^{-16}（有效性概率为6×10^{-8}），而状态感知局部解码可偏差0.97。对比实验显示：观察键控的官方CARS在采样步骤数上更优（root/Stateful比率0.942 [0.934,0.951]），Qwen对比无显著差异（0.99 [0.90,1.08]）。跨历史迁移仅在内部匹配键消融中有帮助（1.27×）。问题规模可能因历史依赖语言模型呈指数增长，因此不声称通用有限trie可扩展性。

Conclusion: 证据支持Stateful CARS提供了由模式诱导的精确条件采样，但在系统层面并未优于现有CARS方法。该方法的价值在于精确性和理论保证，而非系统效率优势。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents face constraints whose meaning changes with observations and prior actions. We study exact sampling from the model distribution conditioned on a hard stateful validator while reusing invalidity certificates across histories. Stateful CARS freezes a bank of sound state--continuation schemas within each attempt and removes every trajectory containing a certified continuation at a matching abstract state. An exact residual Doob transform samples from the resulting proposal. We give a checkable future-validity bisimulation condition, prove schema soundness, adaptive exactness, i.i.d.\ outputs, almost-sure termination, monotone acceptance, and compression invariance, and characterize computation by the number of reachable full-history product states. This number can be exponential for a history-dependent language model; the evaluated method therefore makes no generic finite-trie scalability claim. On enumerable workflows, its analytic law matches the valid conditional to $10^{-16}$ at validity probability $6\times10^{-8}$, whereas state-aware local decoding can be $0.97$ away. A matched comparison is negative: observation-keyed official CARS is cheaper in sampler steps (root/Stateful ratio $0.942$ $[0.934,0.951]$), and the Qwen comparison is null ($0.99$ $[0.90,1.08]$). Cross-history transfer helps only in an internal matched-key ablation ($1.27\times$). Thus the evidence supports exact schema-induced conditioning, not a systems advantage over CARS.

</details>


### [163] [Causal State-Space Model for Causal Inference: Estimating Longitudinal Individual Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08288)
*Abisoye Abidakun,Mingjun Zhong,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSSD和CSSPD两种因果状态空间模型，用于从纵向观测数据估计反事实结果，解决了现有领域混淆（domain confusion）方法中表示不变性与预测准确性之间的互信息冲突问题，并在MIMIC-III和Cancer Simulation数据集上超越Causal Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对抗训练（domain confusion）的反事实推断方法要求表示对治疗分配不变，但这种不变性会抑制与治疗相关的协变量信号，这些信号对准确的结果预测至关重要。这种平衡表示与预测准确性之间的互信息（MI）冲突尚未被正式化，也缺乏结构化的解决方案。

Method: 1) 通过Jensen-Shannon散度界形式化反事实预测误差与会话信息冲突之间的张力；2) CSSD：基于选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba），采用并行多步直接解码器，单次前向传播同时生成所有预测时间步，消除累积 rollout 误差；3) CSSPD：在CSSD基础上引入对比预测编码（CPC）和局部信息最大化（Local InfoMax）正则化，强化平衡表示中的时间可预测性，并恢复被领域混淆破坏的局部协变量信息。

Result: 在MIMIC-III上，CSSPD在每个时间步tau>=2均优于Causal Transformer，在O(T)编码器成本下RMSE降低0.02（2步）至0.07（6步）。在Cancer Simulation上，CSSPD在gamma<=3时优于CT（margin 25.9%-37.0%），CSSD实现最低平均RMSE（相对CT降低12.7%），证实了MI冲突分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 这是首个正式化平衡-预测MI冲突的工作，并通过互补的预测和信息论训练目标提供了结构化解决方案，表明在保持治疗表示不变性的同时恢复局部协变量信息可以显著提升时序反事实预测性能。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual outcomes over time from longitudinal observational data is central to clinical decision support. Existing methods rely on domain confusion -- adversarial training that renders representations invariant to treatment assignment -- yet this invariance creates a mutual information conflict: it suppresses treatment-correlated covariate signals necessary for accurate outcome prediction. We formalise this tension via a Jensen-Shannon divergence bound on counterfactual prediction error and develop two complementary models. CSSD (Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder) adapts selective State Space Models with a parallel multi-step decoder that eliminates accumulated rollout error by producing all prediction horizons simultaneously in a single forward pass. CSSPD (Causal State-Space model with Predictive regularisation and Direct decoder) augments CSSD with Contrastive Predictive Coding and Local Information Maximisation to reinforce temporal predictability in the balancing representation and recover local covariate information destroyed by domain confusion. On MIMIC-III, CSSPD achieves lower counterfactual RMSE than the Causal Transformer at every horizon tau >= 2 at O(T) encoder cost, with gains from 0.02 (2-step) to 0.07 (6-step). On Cancer Simulation across confounding strengths gamma in {0,1,2,3,4}, CSSPD outperforms CT at gamma <= 3 (margins 25.9%--37.0%), and CSSD achieves the lowest overall average RMSE (12.7% reduction over CT), confirming the MI conflict analysis. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formalise the balancing-prediction MI conflict and propose a structured resolution through complementary predictive and information-theoretic training objectives.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将计算论证（computational argumentation）作为评价性AI（Evaluative AI, EAI）的形式化计算基础框架，以支持可解释、可争议的人机协同决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的EAI通过呈现竞争性假设及其正反证据来辅助人类决策，而非给出单一推荐；但当前EAI缺乏形式化、可计算的理论基础，难以实现可解释性和可争议性。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），主张采用计算论证范式为EAI提供形式化、可计算的基础，并构建面向分布式和以人为中心的EAI系统的长期研究议程。

Result: 论文论证了计算论证作为EAI形式化基础的适用性，为后续分布式、人本化EAI研究确立了方向。

Conclusion: 计算论证是支撑可解释、可争议的EAI系统的理想范式，为建立长期分布式及以人为中心的EAI研究议程奠定了基础。

Abstract: Evaluative AI (EAI) has been recently proposed as a way to support human decision-making, not by producing a single recommendation, but by presenting competing hypotheses together with evidence for and against each. In this position paper, we advocate (computational) argumentation as a particularly suitable paradigm to provide a formal, computable foundation for forms of EAI that are explainable and contestable, setting the ground for a long-term research agenda towards distributed and human-centred EAI systems.

</details>


### [165] [Flow-by-Flow:Content-Judgment Bypass for Governing AI Output in High-Loss Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07474)
*Hiroki Naito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Flow-by-Flow"治理范式，指出人机协同监督的结构性瓶颈不是AI输出速度V本身，而是V×L（每项认知负荷）。由于三方面负荷（分诊、判断、回应）对AI能力提升的响应不对称——分诊成本不降、回应成本不变、判断成本仅通过省略而非真实减少来下降——能力提升只会重构而非减少认知负荷。Flow-by-Flow通过基于形式化、可计数特征的认知成本评分与非内容评估的制度容量上限，绕过内容判断来控制监督负荷，并推导出四条设计恒等式。蒙特卡洛分析显示该方法在90.8%的试验中优于单纯强化监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，当AI输出速度V超过人类认知容量C_max时，人在环监督在结构上不可持续。但真正的约束是V×L（每项认知负荷），且L的三个组成部分对AI能力提升的响应不对称，导致能力提升并不能真正减轻人类监督负担。作者旨在提出一种不依赖内容评估的治理范式以解决这一结构性瓶颈。

Method: 论文提出Flow-by-Flow治理框架：1) 基于形式化、可计数特征的认知成本评分，对高批量产出施加非线性成本；2) 制度性容量上限确保处理量保持在C_max以内。同时推导出四条设计恒等式（无内容判断、无审查者能力可扩展消费、身份绑定每应用摩擦、无批量清算），并通过一个参考实现验证其联合可满足性。使用1,000次参数抽取的蒙特卡洛分析评估复合多指标流控相对于单纯监督强化的表现。

Result: 四条设计恒等式被证明可联合满足（参考实现），但实践困难被明确承认。蒙特卡洛分析（1,000次参数抽取）表明，复合多指标流控在90.8%的试验中优于单纯监督强化。

Conclusion: AI能力提升并不减少人类监督的认知负荷，而是重构其结构（从判断转向省略）。由此，基于内容正确性评估的治理机制必然面对V×L天花板或幻觉风险。Flow-by-Flow通过绕开内容判断、以形式化可计数的特征施加流控，为高损失领域的人机治理提供了可行的替代路径。

Abstract: Prior work showed that human-in-the-loop oversight becomes structurally untenable in high-loss domains when AI output velocity V exceeds human cognitive capacity C_max. The operative constraint, however, is not V alone but V x L, where L denotes per-item cognitive load. L consists of triage, judgment, and response, which respond asymmetrically to AI capability improvement. Triage cost does not decline as models become more capable, because semantic indeterminacy is inherent in general-purpose design. Response cost is invariant to accuracy improvements. Only judgment cost faces downward pressure, and this pressure often operates by inducing omission rather than genuine reduction. Capability improvement therefore restructures L rather than reducing it. Governance mechanisms based on evaluating whether AI output is correct either delegate that evaluation to AI and inherit hallucination risk, or delegate it to humans and face the V x L ceiling. We propose Flow-by-Flow, a governance paradigm that controls supervisory load without evaluating content. A cognitive cost score based on formal, countable features imposes nonlinear costs on high-volume production, while an institutional capacity cap keeps processing volume within C_max. We derive four design invariants for any content-judgment-bypass exceedance pathway: no content judgment, no scalable consumption of examiner capacity, identity-bound per-application friction, and no batch clearance. One reference implementation is discussed to show that these invariants are jointly satisfiable, while its practical difficulties are explicitly acknowledged. An illustrative Monte Carlo analysis across 1,000 parameter draws suggests that composite multi-metric flow control outperforms supervision reinforcement alone in 90.8% of trials.

</details>


### [166] [Determinization in Structure Theories: A Unified Framework via Closure, Comparability, and Joint Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07476)
*Hai Hai Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，用于从多元结构理论构建规范解释，区分了三种规范化层级（闭包稳定化、全局完备化、决定性），并将非确定性分类为认识论多元性（E型）和结构多元性（S型），提出了两种规范化机制（算子基完备化和选择子基构造），并证明了相关存在性条件与分类定理，还将其应用于LLM辅助推理中幻觉问题的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多元结构理论中，如何从多个可能的解释中构建"规范的"（canonical）唯一解释是一个基础性问题。现有的理论框架缺乏对规范化过程的系统性形式化描述，导致无法统一理解不同场景下的解释选择问题，包括逻辑推理中的非确定性以及LLM推理中的幻觉现象。

Method: 本文通过三元组T=(Σ,A,I)（签名、公理、推理策略）形式化结构理论，定义可容许解释族为所有全局一致的结构结论赋值。提出了三种规范化层级（闭包稳定化、全局完备化、决定性）和两类非确定性分类（E型和S型，其中S型-强子类由缺乏公共上界刻画）。识别出两种规范化机制：算子基完备化和选择子基构造，并给出了这些机制存在的充分结构条件。证明了纯推理基完备化在正性、非反缩规则及额外可靠性条件下可归结为饱和闭包算子。最后通过分级算子论证了多层级规范化的非交换性，并提出有条件的分类定理。

Result: （1）对E型理论建立了闭包稳定化结果，而完全决定性依赖于一个仍待解决的全局合流性质；（2）对S型-强理论，通过规范选择实现了决定性；（3）证明了多层级规范化通过分级算子形成结构非交换系统；（4）给出了将理论内禀机制归结为闭包或选择的有条件分类定理；（5）将框架应用于LLM辅助推理，将幻觉形式化为"无支持的规范化"。

Conclusion: 该框架为多元结构理论的规范化解释提供了统一的形式化基础，区分了不同类型的非确定性并给出了相应的规范化策略。尽管E型理论的完全决定性条件仍待探索，但该框架已展示了良好的适用性，能够为LLM推理中的幻觉现象提供形式化建模视角。

Abstract: We develop a formal framework for constructing canonical interpretations from plural structure theories. A structure theory is a triple T = (Σ, A, I) consisting of a signature, axioms, and an inference policy, whose admissible interpretation family collects all globally consistent assignments of structural conclusions.
  We distinguish three levels of canonicalization: closure stabilization (per-seed convergence), global completion (seed-independent convergence), and determinization (a unique admissible interpretation). Non-determinism is classified into epistemic plurality (Type E) and structural plurality (Type S), with a refined Type S-strong subclass characterized by the absence of common upper bounds.
